00001         * PACMAIN : DRIVER ONLY. NO GAME RULES, NO I/O STATEMENTS.
00002         * PACPLAT (HOST)  PACINPT (CARDS)  PACGAME (RULES)  PACMAP (MAP)
00003         * PACAI (GHOSTS)  PACDISP (TEXT OUTPUT). SEE DOCS/ARCHITECTURE.MD
00004         * PARM 'TSO' [AIL N] REQUESTS THE INTERACTIVE TSO ADAPTER
00005         * (MVS-8): CONFIG COMES FROM THE PARM, THE BOARD IS SHOWN BEFORE
00006         * THE FIRST PROMPT. NO PARM = BATCH CARD MODE (REFERENCE).
00007         * NO INPUT IS READ ONCE THE GAME HAS ENDED (QUIT, WON, LOST).
00008         * OPTIONAL 'M' IN PARM COLUMN 11 (E.G. 'BAT       M' OR
00009         * 'TSO AI3 2 M') MAKES PACDISP PRINT A FINAL METRICS LINE.
00010         * COLUMN 12 = COLOUR (MVS-G1): A AUTO, Y YES, N/BLANK NO. PARM
00011         * 'TSD ...' IS THE BATCH DRY RUN OF THE COLOUR SCREENS (HEX).
00012         * COLUMN 13 = HIGHLIGHT (MVS-G2): A AUTO, Y YES, N/BLANK NO.
00013         * COLUMN 14 = ANIM (MVS-G3): A AUTO, Y YES, N/BLANK NO.
00014         * COLUMN 15 = REDRAW: P PARTIAL, ANYTHING ELSE FULL (REFERENCE).
00015          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00016          PROGRAM-ID. PACMAIN.
00017          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
00018          CONFIGURATION SECTION.
00019          SOURCE-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00020          OBJECT-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00021          DATA DIVISION.
00022          WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
00023          01  PAC-STATE COPY PACSTATE.
00024 C       * SHARED GAME STATE. NO VALUE CLAUSES (USED IN LINKAGE TOO).
00025 C       * ONLY PACGAME/PACMAP/PACAI MODIFY IT; PACDISP READS IT.
00026 C        01  PAC-STATE.
00027 C            05  MAP-AREA.
00028 C                10  MAP-ROW           OCCURS 10 TIMES.
00029 C                    15  MAP-CELL      PIC X OCCURS 20 TIMES.
00030 C            05  PLAYER-X              PIC 99.
00031 C            05  PLAYER-Y              PIC 99.
00032 C            05  TICK-COUNT            PIC 999.
00033 C            05  SCORE                 PIC 9(4).
00034 C            05  PELLETS-LEFT          PIC 999.
00035 C            05  LIVES                 PIC 9.
00036 C            05  GAME-STATE            PIC X(7).
00037 C            05  LAST-CMD              PIC X.
00038 C            05  LAST-RESULT           PIC X(7).
00039 C            05  F-WON                 PIC 9.
00040 C            05  F-LOST                PIC 9.
00041 C            05  F-QUIT                PIC 9.
00042 C            05  F-EOF                 PIC 9.
00043 C            05  F-HIT                 PIC 9.
00044 C            05  AI-LEVEL              PIC 9.
00045 C            05  GHOST-COUNT           PIC 9.
00046 C            05  GHOST-SEED            PIC 9(5).
00047 C            05  GH-ENTRY              OCCURS 3 TIMES.
00048 C                10  GH-X              PIC 99.
00049 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00050 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00051          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00052 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00053 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00054 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).   2


00055 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00056 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00057 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00058 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00059 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00060 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00061 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).
00062 C            05  REQ-METRIC            PIC X.
00063 C            05  REQ-COLOR             PIC X.
00064 C            05  REQ-INKEY             PIC X.
00065 C            05  REQ-INTEXT.
00066 C                10  REQ-INTEXT-CH     PIC X OCCURS 8 TIMES.
00067 C            05  REQ-HILITE            PIC X.
00068 C            05  REQ-ANIM              PIC X.
00069 C            05  REQ-REDRAW            PIC X.
00070 C            05  REQ-SENDST            PIC X.
00071          01  WS-CFG.
00072              05  WS-CFG-CH         PIC X OCCURS 5 TIMES.
00073          01  WS-K                  PIC 99.
00074          LINKAGE SECTION.
00075          01  PARM-AREA.
00076              05  PARM-LEN          PIC S9(4) COMP.
00077              05  PARM-MODE         PIC X(3).
00078              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00079              05  PARM-CFG          PIC X(5).
00080              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00081              05  PARM-MET          PIC X.
00082              05  PARM-COLOR        PIC X.
00083              05  PARM-HILITE       PIC X.
00084              05  PARM-ANIM         PIC X.
00085              05  PARM-REDRAW       PIC X.
00086          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PARM-AREA.
00087          MAIN-PARA.
00088              MOVE 'BAT' TO REQ-MODE.
00089              MOVE SPACE TO REQ-METRIC.
00090              IF PARM-LEN > 10 AND PARM-MET = 'M'
00091                  MOVE 'M' TO REQ-METRIC.
00092              IF PARM-LEN > 2 AND PARM-MODE = 'TSO'
00093                  MOVE 'TSO' TO REQ-MODE.
00094              IF PARM-LEN > 2 AND PARM-MODE = 'TSD'
00095                  MOVE 'TSD' TO REQ-MODE.
00096              MOVE 'N' TO REQ-COLOR.
00097              IF PARM-LEN > 11
00098                  MOVE PARM-COLOR TO REQ-COLOR.
00099              MOVE 'N' TO REQ-HILITE.
00100              IF PARM-LEN > 12
00101                  MOVE PARM-HILITE TO REQ-HILITE.
00102              MOVE 'N' TO REQ-ANIM.
00103              IF PARM-LEN > 13
00104                  MOVE PARM-ANIM TO REQ-ANIM.
00105              MOVE 'F' TO REQ-REDRAW.
00106              IF PARM-LEN > 14
00107                  MOVE PARM-REDRAW TO REQ-REDRAW.
00108              MOVE SPACE TO REQ-SENDST.
00109              MOVE 'INIT' TO REQ-OP.
00110              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00111              MOVE 'RSET' TO REQ-OP.   3


00112              CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00113              MOVE 'OPEN' TO REQ-OP.
00114              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00115              MOVE 'HEAD' TO REQ-OP.
00116              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00117              IF REQ-MODE = 'BAT'
00118                  PERFORM BAT-START THRU BAT-START-EXIT
00119              ELSE
00120                  PERFORM TSO-START THRU TSO-START-EXIT.
00121              PERFORM TURN-PARA THRU TURN-PARA-EXIT
00122                  UNTIL F-EOF = 1 OR F-QUIT = 1 OR F-WON = 1
00123                     OR F-LOST = 1.
00124              MOVE 'END ' TO REQ-OP.
00125              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00126              MOVE 'CLOS' TO REQ-OP.
00127              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00128              MOVE 'FINI' TO REQ-OP.
00129              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00130              STOP RUN.
00131          BAT-START.
00132              PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00133              PERFORM CHECK-CONFIG THRU CHECK-CONFIG-EXIT.
00134              PERFORM SHOW-START.
00135          BAT-START-EXIT.
00136              EXIT.
00137          TSO-START.
00138              IF PARM-LEN > 4
00139                  PERFORM COPY-PARM-CFG
00140                  MOVE 'PARM' TO REQ-OP
00141                  CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ
00142                  PERFORM APPLY-CONFIG THRU APPLY-CONFIG-EXIT.
00143              PERFORM SHOW-START.
00144              PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00145          TSO-START-EXIT.
00146              EXIT.
00147          COPY-PARM-CFG.
00148         * ONLY PARM-LEN BYTES ARE VALID: BLANK WHAT LIES BEYOND THEM.
00149              MOVE PARM-CFG TO WS-CFG.
00150              COMPUTE WS-K = PARM-LEN - 3.
00151              PERFORM BLANK-CFG-CH UNTIL WS-K > 5.
00152              MOVE WS-CFG TO REQ-TEXT.
00153          BLANK-CFG-CH.
00154              MOVE SPACE TO WS-CFG-CH (WS-K).
00155              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00156          SHOW-START.
00157              MOVE 'INIT' TO REQ-OP.
00158              CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00159              MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP.
00160              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00161          APPLY-CONFIG.
00162              IF REQ-KIND = 'CFG'
00163                  MOVE 'CFG ' TO REQ-OP
00164                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00165          APPLY-CONFIG-EXIT.
00166              EXIT.
00167          READ-INPUT.
00168              MOVE 'PRMT' TO REQ-OP.   4


00169              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00170              MOVE 'READ' TO REQ-OP.
00171              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00172              PERFORM IDLE-TICK UNTIL REQ-KIND NOT = 'TCK'.
00173              IF REQ-KIND = 'EOF'
00174                  MOVE 'EOF ' TO REQ-OP
00175                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00176          READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00177              EXIT.
00178          IDLE-TICK.
00179         * MVS-G3 VISUAL TICK: THE WAIT FOR INPUT WAS INTERRUPTED BY AN
00180         * IDLE TICK. ONLY THE SCREEN CHANGES; NO GAME CALL, NO TURN.
00181              MOVE 'TICK' TO REQ-OP.
00182              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00183              MOVE 'READ' TO REQ-OP.
00184              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00185          CHECK-CONFIG.
00186              IF REQ-KIND = 'CFG'
00187                  MOVE 'CFG ' TO REQ-OP
00188                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00189                  PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00190          CHECK-CONFIG-EXIT.
00191              EXIT.
00192          TURN-PARA.
00193              PERFORM DISPATCH THRU DISPATCH-EXIT.
00194              IF F-QUIT = 0 AND F-WON = 0 AND F-LOST = 0
00195                  PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00196          TURN-PARA-EXIT.
00197              EXIT.
00198          DISPATCH.
00199              IF REQ-KIND = 'MOV'
00200                  MOVE 'MOVE' TO REQ-OP
00201                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00202                  MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP
00203                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00204              ELSE IF REQ-KIND = 'QUI'
00205                  MOVE 'QUIT' TO REQ-OP
00206                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00207              ELSE IF REQ-KIND = 'RDR'
00208                  MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP
00209                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00210              ELSE
00211                  MOVE 'INVL' TO REQ-OP
00212                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00213          DISPATCH-EXIT.
00214              EXIT.   5

00082          MAIN-PARA.
00083              IF REQ-OP = 'RSET'
00084                  PERFORM DO-RESET
00085              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CFG '
00086                  PERFORM DO-CONFIG
00087              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'INIT'
00088                  PERFORM DO-INIT
00089              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'MOVE'
00090                  PERFORM DO-MOVE
00091              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'QUIT'
00092                  PERFORM DO-QUIT
00093              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'EOF '
00094                  MOVE 1 TO F-EOF.
00095              GOBACK.
00096          DO-RESET.
00097              MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-X.
00098              MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-Y.
00099              MOVE 0 TO TICK-COUNT.
00100              MOVE 0 TO SCORE.
00101              MOVE 0 TO PELLETS-LEFT.
00102              MOVE 3 TO LIVES.
00103              MOVE 'READY  ' TO GAME-STATE.
00104              MOVE ' ' TO LAST-CMD.
00105              MOVE 'START  ' TO LAST-RESULT.
00106              MOVE 0 TO F-WON.
00107              MOVE 0 TO F-LOST.
00108              MOVE 0 TO F-QUIT.
00109              MOVE 0 TO F-EOF.
00110              MOVE 0 TO F-HIT.
00111              MOVE 1 TO AI-LEVEL.   3


00112              MOVE 1 TO GHOST-COUNT.
00113              MOVE 12345 TO GHOST-SEED.
00114          DO-CONFIG.
00115              MOVE REQ-LEVEL TO AI-LEVEL.
00116              MOVE REQ-COUNT TO GHOST-COUNT.
00117          DO-INIT.
00118              MOVE 'LOAD' TO M-OP.
00119              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00120              MOVE PLAYER-X TO M-X.
00121              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO M-Y.
00122              MOVE 'CLR ' TO M-OP.
00123              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00124              PERFORM RESET-GHOST VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00125                  UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00126              PERFORM CLEAR-GHOST-CELL VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00127                  UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00128              MOVE 'CNT ' TO M-OP.
00129              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00130              MOVE M-COUNT TO PELLETS-LEFT.
00131          RESET-GHOST.
00132              MOVE GS-X (WS-G) TO GH-X (WS-G).
00133              MOVE GS-Y (WS-G) TO GH-Y (WS-G).
00134              MOVE GS-DIR (WS-G) TO GH-DIR (WS-G).
00135          CLEAR-GHOST-CELL.
00136              MOVE GH-X (WS-G) TO M-X.
00137              MOVE GH-Y (WS-G) TO M-Y.
00138              MOVE 'CLR ' TO M-OP.
00139              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00140          DO-QUIT.
00141              MOVE 1 TO F-QUIT.
00142              MOVE 'QUIT   ' TO GAME-STATE.
00143          DO-MOVE.
00144              MOVE REQ-CHAR TO LAST-CMD.
00145              MOVE PLAYER-X TO NEW-X.
00146              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO NEW-Y.
00147              MOVE 'MOVED  ' TO LAST-RESULT.
00148              PERFORM AIM-MOVE THRU AIM-MOVE-EXIT.
00149              ADD 1 TO TICK-COUNT.
00150              MOVE NEW-X TO M-X.
00151              MOVE NEW-Y TO M-Y.
00152              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00153              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00154              IF M-FLAG = 1
00155                  MOVE 'BLOCKED' TO LAST-RESULT
00156              ELSE
00157                  MOVE NEW-X TO PLAYER-X
00158                  MOVE NEW-Y TO PLAYER-Y
00159                  PERFORM EAT-PELLET THRU EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00160              PERFORM ROUND-END THRU ROUND-END-EXIT.
00161              PERFORM SET-STATE THRU SET-STATE-EXIT.
00162          AIM-MOVE.
00163              IF REQ-DIR = 'U'
00164                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-Y
00165              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'D'
00166                  ADD 1 TO NEW-Y
00167              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'L'
00168                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-X   4


00169              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'R'
00170                  ADD 1 TO NEW-X.
00171          AIM-MOVE-EXIT.
00172              EXIT.
00173          EAT-PELLET.
00174              MOVE PLAYER-X TO M-X.
00175              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO M-Y.
00176              MOVE 'EAT ' TO M-OP.
00177              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00178              IF M-FLAG = 1
00179                  ADD 10 TO SCORE
00180                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM PELLETS-LEFT.
00181              IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00182                  MOVE 1 TO F-WON.
00183          EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00184              EXIT.
00185          ROUND-END.
00186              MOVE 0 TO F-HIT.
00187              PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00188              IF F-HIT = 0 AND F-WON = 0 AND AI-LEVEL > 0
00189                  CALL 'PACAI' USING PAC-STATE
00190                  PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00191              IF F-HIT = 1
00192                  PERFORM LOSE-LIFE THRU LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00193          ROUND-END-EXIT.
00194              EXIT.
00195          CHECK-HIT.
00196              PERFORM HIT-TEST THRU HIT-TEST-EXIT
00197                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00198          CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00199              EXIT.
00200          HIT-TEST.
00201              IF GH-X (WS-G) = PLAYER-X AND GH-Y (WS-G) = PLAYER-Y
00202                  MOVE 1 TO F-HIT.
00203          HIT-TEST-EXIT.
00204              EXIT.
00205          LOSE-LIFE.
00206              MOVE 0 TO F-WON.
00207              SUBTRACT 1 FROM LIVES.
00208              IF LIVES = 0
00209                  MOVE 1 TO F-LOST
00210                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO LAST-RESULT
00211              ELSE
00212                  MOVE 'DIED   ' TO LAST-RESULT
00213                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-X
00214                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-Y
00215                  PERFORM RESET-GHOST VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00216                      UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT
00217                  IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00218                      MOVE 1 TO F-WON.
00219          LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00220              EXIT.
00221          SET-STATE.
00222              IF F-LOST = 1
00223                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO GAME-STATE
00224              ELSE IF F-WON = 1
00225                  MOVE 'WON    ' TO GAME-STATE   5


00226              ELSE
00227                  MOVE 'RUNNING' TO GAME-STATE.
00228          SET-STATE-EXIT.
00229              EXIT.   6

00057          01  MAP-REQ COPY PACMREQ.
00058 C       * MAP SERVICE REQUEST: M-OP = LOAD WALL CLR  EAT  CNT
00059 C        01  MAP-REQ.
00060 C            05  M-OP                  PIC X(4).
00061 C            05  M-X                   PIC 99.
00062 C            05  M-Y                   PIC 99.
00063 C            05  M-FLAG                PIC 9.

00065          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00066          MAIN-PARA.
00067              MOVE 0 TO M-FLAG.
00068              IF M-OP = 'LOAD'
00069                  MOVE MAP-DATA TO MAP-AREA
00070              ELSE IF M-OP = 'WALL'
00071                  PERFORM DO-WALL THRU DO-WALL-EXIT
00072              ELSE IF M-OP = 'CLR '
00073                  MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X)
00074              ELSE IF M-OP = 'EAT '
00075                  PERFORM DO-EAT THRU DO-EAT-EXIT
00076              ELSE IF M-OP = 'CNT '
00077                  PERFORM DO-COUNT.
00078              GOBACK.
00079          DO-WALL.
00080              IF M-X < 1 OR M-X > 20 OR M-Y < 1 OR M-Y > 10
00081                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG
00082              ELSE IF MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X) = '#'
00083                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG.
00084          DO-WALL-EXIT.
00085              EXIT.
00086          DO-EAT.
00087              IF MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X) = '.'
00088                  MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X)
00089                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG.
00090          DO-EAT-EXIT.
00091              EXIT.
00092          DO-COUNT.
00093              MOVE 0 TO M-COUNT.
00094              PERFORM COUNT-ROW VARYING WS-ROW FROM 1 BY 1
00095                  UNTIL WS-ROW > 10.
00096          COUNT-ROW.
00097              PERFORM COUNT-CELL THRU COUNT-CELL-EXIT
00098                  VARYING WS-COL FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-COL > 20.
00099          COUNT-CELL.
00100              IF MAP-CELL (WS-ROW WS-COL) = '.'
00101                  ADD 1 TO M-COUNT.
00102          COUNT-CELL-EXIT.
00103              EXIT.   3

00001         * PACAI : GHOST MOVEMENT FOR ALL ACTIVE GHOSTS (PORTABLE).
00002         * AI-LEVEL 1 RANDOM, 2 PERSISTENT DIRECTION, 3 CHASE (MANHATTAN).
00003         * LCG SEED IN THE STATE (FIXED 12345) KEEPS RUNS REPRODUCIBLE.

00073 C                10  GH-X              PIC 99.
00074 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00075 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00076          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE.
00077          MAIN-PARA.
00078              PERFORM GHOST-MOVE THRU GHOST-MOVE-EXIT
00079                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00080              GOBACK.
00081          GHOST-MOVE.
00082              MOVE 0 TO CAND-N.
00083              MOVE GH-DIR (WS-G) TO WS-CUR.
00084              MOVE REV-D (WS-CUR) TO WS-REVD.
00085              PERFORM GHOST-PROBE THRU GHOST-PROBE-EXIT
00086                  VARYING WS-D FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-D > 4.
00087              IF CAND-N = 0 AND AI-LEVEL > 1
00088                  PERFORM TRY-REVERSE THRU TRY-REVERSE-EXIT.
00089              IF CAND-N > 0
00090                  PERFORM CHOOSE-DIR THRU CHOOSE-DIR-EXIT
00091                  PERFORM STEP-GHOST.
00092          GHOST-MOVE-EXIT.
00093              EXIT.
00094          GHOST-PROBE.
00095              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00096              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00097              MOVE G-NX TO M-X.
00098              MOVE G-NY TO M-Y.
00099              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00100              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00101              IF M-FLAG = 0
00102                  IF AI-LEVEL = 1 OR WS-D NOT = WS-REVD
00103                      ADD 1 TO CAND-N
00104                      MOVE WS-D TO CAND (CAND-N).
00105          GHOST-PROBE-EXIT.
00106              EXIT.
00107          TRY-REVERSE.
00108              MOVE WS-REVD TO WS-D.
00109              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00110              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00111              MOVE G-NX TO M-X.   3


00112              MOVE G-NY TO M-Y.
00113              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00114              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00115              IF M-FLAG = 0
00116                  MOVE 1 TO CAND-N
00117                  MOVE WS-D TO CAND (1).
00118          TRY-REVERSE-EXIT.
00119              EXIT.
00120          CHOOSE-DIR.
00121              IF AI-LEVEL = 3
00122                  PERFORM PICK-CHASE
00123              ELSE IF AI-LEVEL = 2 AND CAND-N = 1
00124                  MOVE CAND (1) TO WS-D
00125              ELSE
00126                  PERFORM PICK-RANDOM.
00127          CHOOSE-DIR-EXIT.
00128              EXIT.
00129          PICK-RANDOM.
00130              COMPUTE WS-WORK = GHOST-SEED * 75 + 74.
00131              DIVIDE WS-WORK BY 65537 GIVING WS-QUOT
00132                  REMAINDER GHOST-SEED.
00133              DIVIDE GHOST-SEED BY CAND-N GIVING WS-QUOT
00134                  REMAINDER WS-REM.
00135              ADD 1 TO WS-REM.
00136              MOVE CAND (WS-REM) TO WS-D.
00137          PICK-CHASE.
00138              MOVE 999 TO BEST-DIST.
00139              PERFORM CHASE-EVAL THRU CHASE-EVAL-EXIT
00140                  VARYING WS-J FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-J > CAND-N.
00141          CHASE-EVAL.
00142              MOVE CAND (WS-J) TO WS-K.
00143              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-K) - 1.
00144              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-K) - 1.
00145              COMPUTE DIST-X = G-NX - PLAYER-X.
00146              IF DIST-X < 0
00147                  COMPUTE DIST-X = 0 - DIST-X.
00148              COMPUTE DIST-Y = G-NY - PLAYER-Y.
00149              IF DIST-Y < 0
00150                  COMPUTE DIST-Y = 0 - DIST-Y.
00151              COMPUTE DIST-T = DIST-X + DIST-Y.
00152              IF DIST-T < BEST-DIST
00153                  MOVE DIST-T TO BEST-DIST
00154                  MOVE WS-K TO WS-D.
00155          CHASE-EVAL-EXIT.
00156              EXIT.
00157          STEP-GHOST.
00158              COMPUTE GH-X (WS-G) = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00159              COMPUTE GH-Y (WS-G) = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00160              MOVE WS-D TO GH-DIR (WS-G).   4

00001         * PACINPT : INPUT ADAPTER. ONE SYSIN CARD -> ONE REQUEST.

00042          01  WS-LIM                PIC 99.
00043          LINKAGE SECTION.
00044          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00045 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00046 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00047 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00048 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00049 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00050 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00051 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00052 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00053 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00054 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).   2


00055 C            05  REQ-METRIC            PIC X.
00056 C            05  REQ-COLOR             PIC X.
00057 C            05  REQ-INKEY             PIC X.
00058 C            05  REQ-INTEXT.
00059 C                10  REQ-INTEXT-CH     PIC X OCCURS 8 TIMES.
00060 C            05  REQ-HILITE            PIC X.
00061 C            05  REQ-ANIM              PIC X.
00062 C            05  REQ-REDRAW            PIC X.
00063 C            05  REQ-SENDST            PIC X.
00064          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-REQ.
00065          MAIN-PARA.
00066              IF REQ-OP = 'OPEN'
00067                  OPEN INPUT COMMAND-FILE
00068              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'READ' AND REQ-MODE = 'TSC'
00069                  PERFORM TSC-READ THRU TSC-READ-EXIT
00070              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'READ'
00071                  PERFORM DO-READ THRU DO-READ-EXIT
00072              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CLOS'
00073                  CLOSE COMMAND-FILE
00074              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'PARM'
00075                  MOVE SPACES TO WS-CARD
00076                  MOVE REQ-TEXT TO WS-CARD
00077                  PERFORM CLASSIFY THRU CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00078              GOBACK.
00079          DO-READ.
00080              MOVE SPACES TO REQ-KIND.
00081              READ COMMAND-FILE AT END MOVE 'EOF' TO REQ-KIND.
00082              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES
00083                  MOVE COMMAND-RECORD TO WS-CARD.
00084              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES AND REQ-MODE NOT = 'BAT'
00085                  PERFORM NORMALIZE THRU NORMALIZE-EXIT.
00086              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES AND REQ-MODE NOT = 'BAT'
00087                                   AND WS-CARD = SPACES
00088                  MOVE 'RDR' TO REQ-KIND.
00089              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES AND REQ-MODE = 'TSD'
00090                                   AND CMD-CHAR = '+'
00091                  MOVE 'TCK' TO REQ-KIND.
00092              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES
00093                  PERFORM CLASSIFY THRU CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00094          DO-READ-EXIT.
00095              EXIT.
00096          TSC-READ.
00097              MOVE 'RECV' TO REQ-OP.
00098              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00099              MOVE 'READ' TO REQ-OP.
00100              IF REQ-INKEY = 'X'
00101                  PERFORM DO-READ THRU DO-READ-EXIT
00102                  GO TO TSC-READ-EXIT.
00103              IF REQ-INKEY = 'T'
00104                  MOVE 'TCK' TO REQ-KIND
00105                  GO TO TSC-READ-EXIT.
00106              MOVE 'RDR' TO REQ-KIND.
00107              IF REQ-INKEY NOT = 'E'
00108                  GO TO TSC-READ-EXIT.
00109              MOVE SPACES TO WS-CARD.
00110              MOVE REQ-INTEXT TO WS-CARD.
00111              PERFORM NORMALIZE THRU NORMALIZE-EXIT.   3


00112              IF WS-CARD NOT = SPACES
00113                  PERFORM CLASSIFY THRU CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00114          TSC-READ-EXIT.
00115              EXIT.
00116          NORMALIZE.
00117         * TSO/TSC ONLY: TOLERATE LEADING BLANKS/NON-PRINTABLE BYTES TYPED
00118         * BEFORE THE COMMAND: START AT THE FIRST BYTE > BLANK (1-8).
00119              MOVE 1 TO WS-OFF.
00120              PERFORM FIND-START UNTIL WS-OFF > 8
00121                  OR RAW-CHAR (WS-OFF) > SPACE.
00122              IF WS-OFF > 1 AND WS-OFF < 9
00123                  PERFORM SHIFT-LEFT THRU SHIFT-LEFT-EXIT.
00124          NORMALIZE-EXIT.
00125              EXIT.
00126          FIND-START.
00127              ADD 1 TO WS-OFF.
00128          SHIFT-LEFT.
00129              MOVE SPACES TO NORM-REC.
00130              COMPUTE WS-LIM = 81 - WS-OFF.
00131              PERFORM COPY-CHAR VARYING WS-I FROM 1 BY 1
00132                  UNTIL WS-I > WS-LIM.
00133              MOVE NORM-REC TO WS-CARD.
00134          SHIFT-LEFT-EXIT.
00135              EXIT.
00136          COPY-CHAR.
00137              COMPUTE WS-J = WS-I + WS-OFF - 1.
00138              MOVE RAW-CHAR (WS-J) TO NR-CHAR (WS-I).
00139          CLASSIFY.
00140              MOVE CMD-CHAR TO REQ-CHAR.
00141              MOVE 'INV' TO REQ-KIND.
00142              IF CMD-CHAR = 'U' OR CMD-CHAR = 'u'
00143                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00144                  MOVE 'U' TO REQ-DIR
00145              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'D' OR CMD-CHAR = 'd'
00146                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00147                  MOVE 'D' TO REQ-DIR
00148              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'L' OR CMD-CHAR = 'l'
00149                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00150                  MOVE 'L' TO REQ-DIR
00151              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'R' OR CMD-CHAR = 'r'
00152                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00153                  MOVE 'R' TO REQ-DIR
00154              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'Q' OR CMD-CHAR = 'q'
00155                  MOVE 'QUI' TO REQ-KIND
00156              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'A'
00157                  MOVE 'CFG' TO REQ-KIND
00158                  PERFORM SET-CONFIG THRU SET-CONFIG-EXIT.
00159          CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00160              EXIT.
00161          SET-CONFIG.
00162              IF CMD-LEVEL = '0'
00163                  MOVE 0 TO REQ-LEVEL
00164              ELSE IF CMD-LEVEL = '2'
00165                  MOVE 2 TO REQ-LEVEL
00166              ELSE IF CMD-LEVEL = '3'
00167                  MOVE 3 TO REQ-LEVEL
00168              ELSE   4


00169                  MOVE 1 TO REQ-LEVEL.
00170              IF CMD-COUNT = '2'
00171                  MOVE 2 TO REQ-COUNT
00172              ELSE IF CMD-COUNT = '3'
00173                  MOVE 3 TO REQ-COUNT
00174              ELSE
00175                  MOVE 1 TO REQ-COUNT.
00176          SET-CONFIG-EXIT.
00177              EXIT.   5

00001         * PACDISP : DISPLAY ADAPTER (PLAIN TEXT, LEVEL G0). READS THE
00002         * STATE, NEVER CHANGES IT.  OPS : HEAD  STAT  INVL  END  PRMT
00003         * PRMT shows the input prompt in TSO mode only (no-op in BAT).

00130          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00131          MAIN-PARA.
00132              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSC' OR REQ-MODE = 'TSD'
00133                  PERFORM SCREEN-OP THRU SCREEN-OP-EXIT
00134              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'HEAD'
00135                  DISPLAY 'PACMAN MVS-6 SMART GHOSTS'
00136                  ADD 1 TO M-LINES
00137                  ADD 25 TO M-CHARS
00138              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'STAT'
00139                  PERFORM SHOW-STATE THRU SHOW-STATE-EXIT
00140              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'INVL'
00141                  DISPLAY 'INVALID COMMAND ' REQ-CHAR
00142                  ADD 1 TO M-LINES
00143                  ADD 17 TO M-CHARS
00144              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'END '
00145                  PERFORM SHOW-END THRU SHOW-END-EXIT
00146              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'PRMT'
00147                  PERFORM SHOW-PROMPT THRU SHOW-PROMPT-EXIT.
00148              GOBACK.
00149          SCREEN-OP.
00150              IF REQ-OP = 'STAT'
00151                  ADD 1 TO M-FRAMES
00152              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'INVL'
00153                  CALL 'PACSCR' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ PAC-DS
00154              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'PRMT'
00155                  PERFORM SCR-PRMT THRU SCR-PRMT-EXIT
00156              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'TICK'
00157                  PERFORM SCR-TICK THRU SCR-TICK-EXIT
00158              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'END '
00159                  MOVE 'FSOF' TO REQ-OP
00160                  CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ
00161                  MOVE 'END ' TO REQ-OP

00163          SCREEN-OP-EXIT.
00164              EXIT.
00165          SCR-PRMT.
00166              CALL 'PACSCR' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ PAC-DS.
00167              ADD DS-LEN TO M-CHARS.
00168              MOVE 'SEND' TO REQ-OP.   4


00169              MOVE SPACE TO REQ-SENDST.
00170              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ PAC-DS.
00171              MOVE 'PRMT' TO REQ-OP.
00172              IF REQ-SENDST = 'R'
00173                  PERFORM SCR-RESEND.
00174          SCR-PRMT-EXIT.
00175              EXIT.
00176          SCR-TICK.
00177         * MVS-G3: VISUAL TICK (GLYPH FRAME ONLY). NO CHANGE = NO SEND.
00178              CALL 'PACSCR' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ PAC-DS.
00179              IF DS-LEN > 0
00180                  ADD DS-LEN TO M-CHARS
00181                  MOVE 'SEND' TO REQ-OP
00182                  MOVE SPACE TO REQ-SENDST
00183                  CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ PAC-DS
00184                  MOVE 'TICK' TO REQ-OP

00186                      PERFORM SCR-RESEND.
00187          SCR-TICK-EXIT.
00188              EXIT.
00189          SCR-RESEND.
00190         * THE PARTIAL SEND FAILED (PACPLAT SWITCHED TO FULL REDRAW, NO
00191         * ANIMATION): SEND THE WHOLE SCREEN INSTEAD.

00209          SHOW-PROMPT-EXIT.
00210              EXIT.
00211          SHOW-END.
00212              IF F-LOST = 1
00213                  DISPLAY 'LOST'

00215              ELSE IF F-WON = 1
00216                  DISPLAY 'WIN'
00217                  ADD 3 TO M-CHARS
00218              ELSE IF F-QUIT = 1
00219                  DISPLAY 'QUIT'

00222                  DISPLAY 'EOF'

00224              DISPLAY 'TURNS ' TICK-COUNT.
00225              DISPLAY 'SCORE ' SCORE.   5


00226              DISPLAY 'LIVES ' LIVES.

00277              EXIT.

00001         * PACPLAT : PLATFORM ADAPTER (MVS 3.8J). OPS : INIT FINI CLS
00002         * SEND RECV FSOF.  INIT validates REQ-MODE (BAT, TSO, TSD) and,
00003         * for TSO with REQ-COLOR A(UTO)/Y(ES), tries the TSC colour mode:
00004         * full-screen on (PACFSIO FSON); AUTO also needs QCode X'86' in
00005         * the Query Reply. Any failure leaves TSO line mode (monochrome
00006         * fallback). SEND/RECV move one TSC screen / one inbound; in TSD
00007         * (batch dry run) SEND prints the data stream in hex. An error in
00008         * TSC restores line mode and falls back to TSO. FSOF and FINI
00009         * always restore line mode if full-screen is active (PACFSIO
00010         * also restores it from an ESTAE exit on abend). CLS (TSO only)
00011         * starts a fresh line-mode screen via PACTSO. GAME/MAP/AI never
00012         * see any of this. MVS-G2: REQ-HILITE A/Y/N IN, Y/N OUT
00013         * (HIGHLIGHT = QCODE X'87' IN THE QUERY REPLY).

00031              05  HX-Z              PIC X VALUE LOW-VALUE.
00032              05  HX-B              PIC X.
00033          01  HEXN REDEFINES HEXW   PIC 9(4) COMP.
00034          01  HEX-DATA              PIC X(16) VALUE '0123456789ABCDEF'.
00035          01  HEX-TABLE REDEFINES HEX-DATA.
00036              05  HEXDIG            PIC X OCCURS 16 TIMES.
00037          01  HEX-LINE.
00038              05  FILLER            PIC X(4) VALUE 'TSD '.
00039              05  HL-CH             PIC X OCCURS 64 TIMES.
00040          01  LEN-LINE.
00041              05  FILLER            PIC X(13) VALUE 'TSD FRAME LEN'.
00042              05  LL-LEN            PIC 9(4).
00043          01  WS-K                  PIC 9(4).
00044          01  WS-P                  PIC 9(4).
00045          01  WS-V                  PIC 9(4).
00046          01  WS-L                  PIC 9(4).
00047          01  WS-ID                 PIC 9(4).
00048          01  WS-QC                 PIC 9(4).
00049          01  WS-LIM                PIC 9(4).
00050          01  WS-O                  PIC 99.
00051          01  WS-Q                  PIC 99.
00052          01  WS-R                  PIC 99.
00053          01  WS-I                  PIC 99.
00054          01  WS-STOP               PIC 9.   2


00055          01  HAS-COLOUR            PIC 9.
00056          01  HAS-HILITE            PIC 9.
00057          01  WS-IDLE               PIC 9(4) VALUE 0.
00058          LINKAGE SECTION.
00059          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00060 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00061 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00062 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00063 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00064 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00065 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00066 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00067 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00068 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00069 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).
00070 C            05  REQ-METRIC            PIC X.
00071 C            05  REQ-COLOR             PIC X.
00072 C            05  REQ-INKEY             PIC X.
00073 C            05  REQ-INTEXT.
00074 C                10  REQ-INTEXT-CH     PIC X OCCURS 8 TIMES.
00075 C            05  REQ-HILITE            PIC X.
00076 C            05  REQ-ANIM              PIC X.
00077 C            05  REQ-REDRAW            PIC X.
00078 C            05  REQ-SENDST            PIC X.
00079          01  PAC-DS COPY PACDS.
00080 C       * 3270 DATA STREAM BUFFER PASSED FROM PACSCR TO PACPLAT (SEND).
00081 C        01  PAC-DS.
00082 C            05  DS-LEN                PIC S9(4) COMP.
00083 C            05  DS-BUF.
00084 C                10  DS-BYTE           PIC X OCCURS 2048 TIMES.
00085 C            05  DS-KIND               PIC X.
00086          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-REQ PAC-DS.
00087          MAIN-PARA.
00088              IF REQ-OP = 'INIT'
00089                  PERFORM DO-INIT THRU DO-INIT-EXIT
00090              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'FINI'
00091                  PERFORM DO-FSOF THRU DO-FSOF-EXIT
00092                  MOVE 0 TO RETURN-CODE
00093              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CLS ' AND REQ-MODE = 'TSO'
00094                  CALL 'PACTSO' USING TSO-OP TSO-RC
00095              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'SEND'
00096                  PERFORM DO-SEND THRU DO-SEND-EXIT
00097              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'RECV'
00098                  PERFORM DO-RECV THRU DO-RECV-EXIT
00099              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'FSOF'
00100                  PERFORM DO-FSOF THRU DO-FSOF-EXIT.
00101              GOBACK.
00102          DO-INIT.
00103              IF REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSO' AND REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSD'
00104                  MOVE 'BAT' TO REQ-MODE.
00105              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSO'
00106                      AND (REQ-COLOR = 'A' OR REQ-COLOR = 'Y')
00107                  PERFORM TRY-COLOUR THRU TRY-COLOUR-EXIT.
00108         * MVS-G2: HIGHLIGHT ONLY IN TSC (OR THE TSD DRY RUN) AND ONLY WHEN
00109         * ASKED: Y, OR A WITH QCODE X'87' IN THE QUERY REPLY (SET IN
00110         * TRY-COLOUR). ANYTHING ELSE IS N.
00111              IF REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSC' AND REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSD'   3


00112                  MOVE 'N' TO REQ-HILITE.
00113              IF REQ-HILITE NOT = 'Y'
00114                  MOVE 'N' TO REQ-HILITE.
00115         * MVS-G3: ANIM Y (A BECOMES Y IN TSC: PARTIAL SEND IS PROVEN,
00116         * SO THERE IS NOTHING TO DETECT), REDRAW P (PARTIAL) ONLY IN
00117         * TSC/TSD; EVERYTHING ELSE IS ANIM N / REDRAW F (FULL).
00118              IF REQ-ANIM = 'A' AND REQ-MODE = 'TSC'
00119                  MOVE 'Y' TO REQ-ANIM.
00120              IF REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSC' AND REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSD'
00121                  MOVE 'N' TO REQ-ANIM
00122                  MOVE 'F' TO REQ-REDRAW.
00123              IF REQ-ANIM NOT = 'Y'
00124                  MOVE 'N' TO REQ-ANIM.
00125              IF REQ-REDRAW NOT = 'P'
00126                  MOVE 'F' TO REQ-REDRAW.
00127          DO-INIT-EXIT.
00128              EXIT.
00129          TRY-COLOUR.
00130              MOVE 'FSON' TO FS-OP.
00131              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP IN-AREA FS-LEN FS-RC.
00132              IF FS-RC NOT = 0
00133                  GO TO TRY-COLOUR-EXIT.
00134              MOVE 1 TO FS-ACTIVE.
00135              IF REQ-COLOR = 'Y'
00136                  MOVE 'TSC' TO REQ-MODE
00137                  GO TO TRY-COLOUR-EXIT.
00138              MOVE 'QRY ' TO FS-OP.
00139              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP IN-AREA FS-LEN FS-RC.
00140              MOVE 0 TO HAS-COLOUR.
00141              MOVE 0 TO HAS-HILITE.
00142              IF FS-RC = 0
00143                  PERFORM FIND-COLOUR THRU FIND-COLOUR-EXIT.
00144              IF HAS-COLOUR = 1 AND HAS-HILITE = 1 AND REQ-HILITE = 'A'
00145                  MOVE 'Y' TO REQ-HILITE.
00146              IF HAS-COLOUR = 1
00147                  MOVE 'TSC' TO REQ-MODE
00148              ELSE
00149                  PERFORM DO-FSOF THRU DO-FSOF-EXIT.
00150          TRY-COLOUR-EXIT.
00151              EXIT.
00152          FIND-COLOUR.
00153         * QUERY REPLY = AID X'88' (136) + STRUCTURED FIELDS: LENGTH(2),
00154         * X'81' (129), QCODE. COLOUR SUPPORT = QCODE X'86' (134).
00155              MOVE 1 TO WS-K.
00156              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00157              IF WS-V NOT = 136
00158                  GO TO FIND-COLOUR-EXIT.
00159              MOVE 2 TO WS-P.
00160              MOVE 0 TO WS-STOP.
00161              PERFORM NEXT-SF THRU NEXT-SF-EXIT UNTIL WS-STOP = 1.
00162          FIND-COLOUR-EXIT.
00163              EXIT.
00164          NEXT-SF.
00165              COMPUTE WS-LIM = WS-P + 3.
00166              IF WS-LIM > FS-LEN
00167                  MOVE 1 TO WS-STOP
00168                  GO TO NEXT-SF-EXIT.   4


00169              MOVE WS-P TO WS-K.
00170              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00171              COMPUTE WS-L = WS-V * 256.
00172              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00173              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00174              ADD WS-V TO WS-L.
00175              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00176              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00177              MOVE WS-V TO WS-ID.
00178              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00179              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00180              MOVE WS-V TO WS-QC.
00181              IF WS-ID = 129 AND WS-QC = 134
00182                  MOVE 1 TO HAS-COLOUR.
00183              IF WS-ID = 129 AND WS-QC = 135
00184                  MOVE 1 TO HAS-HILITE.
00185              IF WS-L < 4
00186                  MOVE 1 TO WS-STOP
00187              ELSE
00188                  ADD WS-L TO WS-P.
00189          NEXT-SF-EXIT.
00190              EXIT.
00191          DO-SEND.
00192              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSD'
00193                  PERFORM PRINT-STREAM THRU PRINT-STREAM-EXIT
00194                  GO TO DO-SEND-EXIT.
00195              IF REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSC'
00196                  GO TO DO-SEND-EXIT.
00197              IF DS-KIND NOT = 'F'
00198                  PERFORM SEND-PART THRU SEND-PART-EXIT
00199                  GO TO DO-SEND-EXIT.
00200              MOVE 'SEND' TO FS-OP.
00201              MOVE DS-LEN TO FS-LEN.
00202              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP DS-BUF FS-LEN FS-RC.
00203              IF FS-RC NOT = 0
00204                  PERFORM FALL-BACK.
00205          DO-SEND-EXIT.
00206              EXIT.
00207          SEND-PART.
00208         * MVS-G3 PARTIAL SEND (WRITE X'F1' + TPUT NOEDIT, PROBE E6). A
00209         * FAILURE IS NOT FATAL: FULL REDRAW WITHOUT ANIMATION FROM NOW ON
00210         * AND THE CALLER IS TOLD TO SEND THE WHOLE SCREEN (REQ-SENDST R).
00211              MOVE 'SENP' TO FS-OP.
00212              MOVE DS-LEN TO FS-LEN.
00213              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP DS-BUF FS-LEN FS-RC.
00214              IF FS-RC NOT = 0
00215                  MOVE 'R' TO REQ-SENDST
00216                  MOVE 'F' TO REQ-REDRAW
00217                  MOVE 'N' TO REQ-ANIM.
00218          SEND-PART-EXIT.
00219              EXIT.
00220          DO-RECV.
00221         * REQ-INKEY: E = ENTER (REQ-INTEXT = TYPED FIELD), P = ANY OTHER
00222         * AID (PA2 RESHOW ...), X = ERROR (NOW BACK IN TSO LINE MODE).
00223              MOVE SPACES TO REQ-INTEXT.
00224              MOVE 'X' TO REQ-INKEY.
00225              IF REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSC'   5


00226                  GO TO DO-RECV-EXIT.
00227              MOVE 'RECV' TO FS-OP.
00228              IF REQ-ANIM = 'Y' AND REQ-REDRAW = 'P'
00229                  MOVE 'RECT' TO FS-OP.
00230              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP IN-AREA FS-LEN FS-RC.
00231              IF FS-RC NOT = 0
00232                  PERFORM FALL-BACK
00233                  GO TO DO-RECV-EXIT.
00234         * MVS-G3 IDLE TICK: RECT = ONE READ + 0.5 S WAIT. EMPTY = TELL THE
00235         * CALLER (REQ-INKEY T); AFTER 5 MINUTES OF NOTHING AS BEFORE.
00236              MOVE 0 TO WS-STOP.
00237              IF FS-OP = 'RECT' AND FS-LEN < 1
00238                  ADD 1 TO WS-IDLE
00239                  MOVE 'T' TO REQ-INKEY
00240                  MOVE 1 TO WS-STOP.
00241              IF WS-STOP = 1 AND WS-IDLE > 599
00242                  PERFORM FALL-BACK
00243                  MOVE 'X' TO REQ-INKEY.
00244              IF WS-STOP = 1
00245                  GO TO DO-RECV-EXIT.
00246              MOVE 0 TO WS-IDLE.
00247              IF FS-LEN < 1
00248                  PERFORM FALL-BACK
00249                  GO TO DO-RECV-EXIT.
00250              MOVE 'P' TO REQ-INKEY.
00251              MOVE 1 TO WS-K.
00252              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00253              IF WS-V NOT = 125
00254                  GO TO DO-RECV-EXIT.
00255              MOVE 'E' TO REQ-INKEY.
00256         * READ MODIFIED: AID, CURSOR(2), SBA X'11'(17) + ADDRESS(2), DATA.
00257              MOVE 4 TO WS-K.
00258              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00259              IF FS-LEN > 6 AND WS-V = 17
00260                  PERFORM COPY-TEXT THRU COPY-TEXT-EXIT
00261                      VARYING WS-I FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-I > 8.
00262          DO-RECV-EXIT.
00263              EXIT.
00264          COPY-TEXT.
00265              COMPUTE WS-K = WS-I + 6.
00266              IF WS-K NOT > FS-LEN
00267                  MOVE IN-CH (WS-K) TO REQ-INTEXT-CH (WS-I).
00268          COPY-TEXT-EXIT.
00269              EXIT.
00270          DO-FSOF.
00271              IF FS-ACTIVE = 1
00272                  MOVE 'FSOF' TO FS-OP
00273                  CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP IN-AREA FS-LEN FS-RC
00274                  MOVE 0 TO FS-ACTIVE.
00275          DO-FSOF-EXIT.
00276              EXIT.
00277          FALL-BACK.
00278              PERFORM DO-FSOF THRU DO-FSOF-EXIT.
00279              MOVE 'TSO' TO REQ-MODE.
00280              MOVE 'N' TO REQ-HILITE.
00281              MOVE 'N' TO REQ-ANIM.
00282              MOVE 'F' TO REQ-REDRAW.   6


00283          BYTE-VAL.
00284              MOVE IN-CH (WS-K) TO HX-B.
00285              MOVE HEXN TO WS-V.
00286          PRINT-STREAM.
00287         * TSD DRY RUN: THE WHOLE DATA STREAM, 32 BYTES PER LINE IN HEX.
00288              MOVE DS-LEN TO LL-LEN.
00289              DISPLAY LEN-LINE.
00290              MOVE 1 TO WS-K.
00291              PERFORM PRINT-LINE UNTIL WS-K > DS-LEN.
00292          PRINT-STREAM-EXIT.
00293              EXIT.
00294          PRINT-LINE.
00295              MOVE SPACES TO HEX-LINE.
00296              MOVE 'TSD ' TO HEX-LINE.
00297              MOVE 0 TO WS-O.
00298              PERFORM PRINT-BYTE UNTIL WS-O > 62 OR WS-K > DS-LEN.
00299              DISPLAY HEX-LINE.
00300          PRINT-BYTE.
00301              MOVE DS-BYTE (WS-K) TO HX-B.
00302              DIVIDE HEXN BY 16 GIVING WS-Q REMAINDER WS-R.
00303              ADD 1 TO WS-Q.
00304              ADD 1 TO WS-R.
00305              ADD 1 TO WS-O.
00306              MOVE HEXDIG (WS-Q) TO HL-CH (WS-O).
00307              ADD 1 TO WS-O.
00308              MOVE HEXDIG (WS-R) TO HL-CH (WS-O).
00309              ADD 1 TO WS-K.   7

00217          01  PAC-DS COPY PACDS.
00218 C       * 3270 DATA STREAM BUFFER PASSED FROM PACSCR TO PACPLAT (SEND).
00219 C        01  PAC-DS.
00220 C            05  DS-LEN                PIC S9(4) COMP.
00221 C            05  DS-BUF.
00222 C                10  DS-BYTE           PIC X OCCURS 2048 TIMES.
00223 C            05  DS-KIND               PIC X.
00224          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ PAC-DS.
00225          MAIN-PARA.   5


00226              IF REQ-OP = 'INVL'
00227                  MOVE 1 TO MSG-ON
00228                  MOVE REQ-CHAR TO INV-CHAR
00229              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'PRMT'
00230                  PERFORM DO-PRMT THRU DO-PRMT-EXIT
00231              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'TICK'
00232                  PERFORM DO-TICK THRU DO-TICK-EXIT
00233              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'FULL'
00234                  PERFORM DO-FULL THRU DO-FULL-EXIT.
00235              GOBACK.
00236          DO-PRMT.
00237              MOVE 0 TO DS-LEN.
00238              MOVE 'F' TO DS-KIND.
00239              PERFORM INIT-HI.
00240              IF REQ-ANIM = 'Y'
00241                  PERFORM TOGGLE-VIS.
00242              PERFORM SET-GLYPHS.
00243              IF REQ-REDRAW = 'P'
00244                  PERFORM PRMT-VS
00245              ELSE
00246                  PERFORM COMPOSE.
00247              MOVE MSG-ON TO MSG-LAST.
00248              MOVE 0 TO MSG-ON.
00249          DO-PRMT-EXIT.
00250              EXIT.
00251          PRMT-VS.
00252         * VIRTUAL IMAGE FIRST; FULL SCREEN WHEN THE TERMINAL HAS NOTHING
00253         * KNOWN (FIRST SCREEN) OR THE LAST INPUT WAS A REDRAW REQUEST.
00254              PERFORM BUILD-VS.
00255              IF PREV-OK = 1 AND REQ-KIND NOT = 'RDR'
00256                  PERFORM BUILD-PARTIAL.
00257              IF PREV-OK = 0 OR REQ-KIND = 'RDR'
00258                  PERFORM COMPOSE.
00259              PERFORM KEEP-VS.
00260          DO-FULL.
00261              MOVE 0 TO DS-LEN.
00262              PERFORM INIT-HI.
00263              PERFORM SET-GLYPHS.
00264              MOVE MSG-LAST TO MSG-ON.
00265              PERFORM COMPOSE.
00266              MOVE 0 TO MSG-ON.
00267              MOVE 0 TO PREV-OK.
00268          DO-FULL-EXIT.
00269              EXIT.
00270          DO-TICK.
00271              MOVE 0 TO DS-LEN.
00272              IF REQ-ANIM = 'Y' AND REQ-REDRAW = 'P' AND PREV-OK = 1
00273                  PERFORM TICK-BUILD.
00274          DO-TICK-EXIT.
00275              EXIT.
00276          INIT-HI.
00277              MOVE 0 TO WS-HP WS-HG WS-HE WS-HN.
00278              IF REQ-HILITE = 'Y'
00279                  MOVE HI-PLAYER TO WS-HP
00280                  MOVE HI-GHOST TO WS-HG
00281                  MOVE HI-ERROR TO WS-HE
00282                  MOVE HI-INPUT TO WS-HN.   6


00283              MOVE 0 TO WS-NOP.
00284          TOGGLE-VIS.
00285              IF VIS = 1
00286                  MOVE 0 TO VIS
00287              ELSE
00288                  MOVE 1 TO VIS.
00289              MOVE 0 TO WS-NOP.
00290          SET-GLYPHS.
00291              MOVE 'P' TO WS-GP.
00292              MOVE 'G' TO WS-GG.
00293              IF REQ-ANIM = 'Y' AND VIS = 0
00294                  MOVE GLY-PLAYER-1 TO WS-GP
00295                  MOVE GLY-GHOST-1 TO WS-GG.
00296              IF REQ-ANIM = 'Y' AND VIS = 1
00297                  MOVE GLY-PLAYER-2 TO WS-GP
00298                  MOVE GLY-GHOST-2 TO WS-GG.
00299              MOVE 0 TO WS-NOP.
00300          BUILD-VS.
00301              MOVE SPACES TO CU-CH-G.
00302              MOVE ALL '0' TO CU-CO-G.
00303              MOVE ALL '0' TO CU-HI-G.
00304              MOVE 1 TO VS-MODE.
00305              PERFORM COMPOSE-BODY.
00306              MOVE 0 TO VS-MODE.
00307          KEEP-VS.
00308              MOVE VS-CUR TO VS-PREV.
00309              MOVE 1 TO PREV-OK.
00310          BUILD-PARTIAL.
00311         * LOGICAL PARTIAL SCREEN: WCC (RESET MDT, RESTORE KEYBOARD), THE
00312         * CHANGED CELLS, THEN THE EMPTY INPUT FIELD WITH THE CURSOR.
00313              MOVE 0 TO DS-LEN.
00314              MOVE 'P' TO DS-KIND.
00315              MOVE 195 TO WS-BV.
00316              PERFORM PUT-BYTE.
00317              PERFORM DIFF-ALL.
00318              PERFORM PUT-FIELD-PART.
00319              PERFORM PUT-TAIL.
00320          TICK-BUILD.
00321         * VISUAL TICK: SAME IMAGE, OTHER GLYPH FRAME. WCC WITHOUT RESET
00322         * MDT SO THAT WHAT THE USER IS TYPING IS NOT LOST.
00323              PERFORM TOGGLE-VIS.
00324              PERFORM SET-GLYPHS.
00325              MOVE VS-PREV TO VS-CUR.
00326              PERFORM FLIP-ROW VARYING WS-I FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-I > 10.
00327              MOVE 'T' TO DS-KIND.
00328              MOVE 194 TO WS-BV.
00329              PERFORM PUT-BYTE.
00330              PERFORM DIFF-ALL.
00331              IF WS-CHG = 0
00332                  MOVE 0 TO DS-LEN.
00333              IF WS-CHG NOT = 0
00334                  PERFORM PUT-TAIL.
00335              PERFORM KEEP-VS.
00336          FLIP-ROW.
00337              PERFORM FLIP-CELL VARYING WS-COL FROM 1 BY 1
00338                  UNTIL WS-COL > 20.
00339          FLIP-CELL.   7


00340              COMPUTE WS-X = (LAY-BOARD-ROW + WS-I - 2) * 80
00341                  + LAY-BOARD-COL + WS-COL - 1.
00342              IF CU-CH (WS-X) = GLY-PLAYER-1
00343                             OR CU-CH (WS-X) = GLY-PLAYER-2
00344                  MOVE WS-GP TO CU-CH (WS-X).
00345              IF CU-CH (WS-X) = GLY-GHOST-1
00346                             OR CU-CH (WS-X) = GLY-GHOST-2
00347                  MOVE WS-GG TO CU-CH (WS-X).
00348              MOVE 0 TO WS-NOP.
00349          DIFF-ALL.
00350              MOVE 0 TO IN-RUN.
00351              MOVE 0 TO RUN-CO.
00352              MOVE 0 TO RUN-HI.
00353              MOVE 0 TO WS-CHG.
00354              PERFORM DIFF-CELL VARYING WS-X FROM 1 BY 1
00355                  UNTIL WS-X > 1920.
00356          DIFF-CELL.
00357              MOVE 0 TO WS-HV.
00358              IF CU-CH (WS-X) NOT = PV-CH (WS-X)
00359                             OR CU-CO (WS-X) NOT = PV-CO (WS-X)
00360                             OR CU-HI (WS-X) NOT = PV-HI (WS-X)
00361                  MOVE 1 TO WS-HV.
00362              IF WS-HV = 0
00363                  MOVE 0 TO IN-RUN.
00364              IF WS-HV = 1
00365                  PERFORM EMIT-CELL.
00366              MOVE 0 TO WS-NOP.
00367          EMIT-CELL.
00368              ADD 1 TO WS-CHG.
00369              COMPUTE WS-ADDR = WS-X - 1.
00370              DIVIDE WS-ADDR BY 80 GIVING WS-ROW REMAINDER WS-COL.
00371              ADD 1 TO WS-ROW.
00372              ADD 1 TO WS-COL.
00373              IF IN-RUN = 0 OR WS-COL = 1
00374                  PERFORM PUT-SBA.
00375              MOVE 1 TO IN-RUN.
00376              IF CU-CO (WS-X) NOT = RUN-CO
00377                  MOVE CU-CO (WS-X) TO RUN-CO
00378                  MOVE RUN-CO TO WS-BV
00379                  PERFORM PUT-COLOUR.
00380              IF CU-HI (WS-X) NOT = RUN-HI
00381                  MOVE CU-HI (WS-X) TO RUN-HI
00382                  MOVE RUN-HI TO WS-HV
00383                  PERFORM PUT-HILITE.
00384              ADD 1 TO DS-LEN.
00385              MOVE CU-CH (WS-X) TO DS-BYTE (DS-LEN).
00386          PUT-FIELD-PART.
00387         * INPUT FIELD DATA BYTE (ROW 18, COL 15): BLANK AGAIN, CURSOR IN.
00388              MOVE LAY-CMD-ROW TO WS-ROW.
00389              MOVE 15 TO WS-COL.
00390              PERFORM PUT-SBA.
00391              MOVE 19 TO WS-BV.
00392              PERFORM PUT-BYTE.
00393              MOVE PAL-HELP TO WS-BV.
00394              PERFORM PUT-COLOUR.
00395              IF WS-HN NOT = 0
00396                  MOVE WS-HN TO WS-HV   8


00397                  PERFORM PUT-HILITE.
00398              IF WS-HN = 0 AND RUN-HI NOT = 0
00399                  MOVE 0 TO WS-HV
00400                  PERFORM PUT-HILITE.
00401              MOVE 64 TO WS-BV.
00402              PERFORM PUT-BYTE.
00403              IF WS-HN NOT = 0
00404                  MOVE 0 TO WS-HV
00405                  PERFORM PUT-HILITE.
00406              MOVE 0 TO WS-NOP.
00407          PUT-TAIL.
00408              MOVE 40 TO WS-BV.
00409              PERFORM PUT-BYTE.
00410              MOVE 0 TO WS-BV.
00411              PERFORM PUT-BYTE.
00412              PERFORM PUT-BYTE.
00413          COMPOSE.
00414              MOVE 0 TO DS-LEN.
00415              MOVE 'F' TO DS-KIND.
00416              MOVE 195 TO WS-BV.
00417              PERFORM PUT-BYTE.
00418              MOVE 1 TO WS-ROW.
00419              MOVE 1 TO WS-COL.
00420              PERFORM PUT-SBA.
00421              MOVE 29 TO WS-BV.
00422              PERFORM PUT-BYTE.
00423              MOVE 96 TO WS-BV.
00424              PERFORM PUT-BYTE.

00426              PERFORM PUT-TAIL.

00430                  PERFORM SHOW-GHOSTS.

00452              MOVE 2 TO WS-COL.
00453              PERFORM PUT-SBA.   9

00457              MOVE 61 TO WS-TLEN.

00459          SHOW-GHOSTS.
00460              MOVE GH-X (2) TO X23-2X.
00461              MOVE GH-Y (2) TO X23-2Y.

00475          SHOW-BOARD-ROW.
00476         * CELL TEXT AS IN PACDISP (PLAYER, THEN GHOSTS IN INDEX ORDER).
00477              MOVE MAP-ROW (WS-I) TO WS-LINE.

00479                  VARYING WS-COL FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-COL > 20.
00480              IF WS-I = PLAYER-Y

00495                      UNTIL WS-COL > 20

PACTSO    SD  0001 000000 0000AC                                                                                                               PAGE    2


  LOC  OBJECT CODE    ADDR1 ADDR2  STMT   SOURCE STATEMENT                                       ASM 0201 17.20 10/08/26

                                      1 *        PACTSO : TSO LINE-MODE SCREEN SERVICE FOR PACPLAT (MVS-8).
                                      2 *        CALL 'PACTSO' USING OP RC
                                      3 *          OP X(4)  'CLS ' = NEXT LINE-MODE OUTPUT STARTS AT LINE 1
                                      4 *                   OF A FRESH SCREEN (STLINENO LINE=1), SO A FRAME

                                      1 *        PACFSIO : TSO FULL-SCREEN SERVICE FOR PACPLAT (MVS-G1, TSC).
                                      2 *        PROTOCOL VALIDATED BY PROBES E2-E5 (DOCS/EVIDENCE/G1-*).
                                      3 *        CALL 'PACFSIO' USING OP BUF LEN RC
                                      4 *          OP  X(4)  FSON  TCLEARQ, STFSMODE ON, STTMPMD ON, ESTAE
                                      5 *                    QRY   READ PARTITION QUERY (TPUT NOEDIT WSF),
                                      6 *                          REPLY INTO BUF/LEN (TGET ASIS)
                                      7 *                    SEND  TPUT FULLSCR OF BUF (LEN BYTES, STARTING
                                      8 *                          WITH THE WCC) WITH A CLEAR-BUFFER PREFIX
                                      9 *                          AFTER THE WCC: SBA(1,1) + REPEAT TO ADDRESS
                                     10 *                          (1,1) WITH X'00' = WHOLE BUFFER NULLED.
                                     11 *                          (ERASE/WRITE X'F5' SENT WITH TPUT NOEDIT
                                     12 *                          LEFT THE SCREEN BLACK, JOB 159.)
                                     13 *                    SENP  MVS-G3 PARTIAL SEND: X'F1' (WRITE, NO ERASE)
                                     14 *                          + BUF (LEN BYTES, STARTING WITH THE WCC),
                                     15 *                          TPUT NOEDIT (PROBE E6, VARIANT W)
                                     16 *                    RECV  TGET ASIS INTO BUF (RETRY EMPTY READS)
                                     17 *                    RECT  ONE TGET ASIS,NOWAIT (R15: 0 INPUT, 4 NONE,
                                     18 *                          ELSE ERROR); NONE = WAIT 0.5 S, LEN 0, RC 0
                                     19 *                          (VISUAL TICK FOR THE CALLER)
                                     20 *                    FSOF  ESTAE CANCEL, STTMPMD OFF, STFSMODE OFF,
                                     21 *                          STLINENO LINE=1 (LINE MODE RESTORED)
                                     22 *          BUF X(2048)   LEN S9(4) COMP   RC S9(4) COMP
                                     23 *          RC: 0 OK, 4 NOT A TSO SESSION (NOTHING ISSUED), 8 UNKNOWN
                                     24 *              OP OR NO INPUT, ELSE THE TPUT/TGET RETURN CODE.
                                     25 *        ABEND SAFETY: FSON ESTABLISHES AN ESTAE EXIT THAT RESTORES
